        <!-- LCP: as `defer` scripts these queued behind every other defer bundle and
             only ran at DOMContentLoaded, which put 2s+ of "element render delay" in
             front of the banner (often the LCP element). The TCF stub is inlined
             (vendored from https://cdn.cookielaw.org/consent/tcf.stub.js) so it runs
             during parse, which frees the SDK stub to be `async` without breaking
             the required stub-before-SDK execution order.
             Vendored 2026-08-01; upstream sha256 was
             0a3a853e4da6234c1518a1ead9bfe98f6d17de9e33201838f742c2ca27d3d8a5 — run
             `curl -s https://cdn.cookielaw.org/consent/tcf.stub.js | shasum -a 256`
             and re-vendor if it changed and TCF/EU consent looks stale. -->
        <script type="text/javascript">var OneTrustTCFStub=(e=>{var t=function(){var s=this;this.LOCATOR_NAME="__tcfapiLocator",this.win=window,this.init=function(){for(;s.win;){try{if(s.win.frames[s.LOCATOR_NAME]){s.cmpFrame=s.win;break}}catch(e){}if(s.win===window.top)break;s.win=s.win.parent}s.cmpFrame||(s.addFrame(),s.win.__tcfapi=s.executeTcfApi,s.win.receiveOTMessage=s.receiveIabMessage,(s.win.attachEvent||s.win.addEventListener)("message",s.win.receiveOTMessage,!1))},this.addFrame=function(){var e,t=s.win.document,i=!!s.win.frames[s.LOCATOR_NAME];return i||(t.body?((e=t.createElement("iframe")).style.cssText="display:none",e.name=s.LOCATOR_NAME,e.setAttribute("title","TCF Locator"),t.body.appendChild(e)):setTimeout(s.addFrame,5)),!i},this.receiveIabMessage=function(i){var a,n="string"==typeof i.data,e={};try{e=n?JSON.parse(i.data):i.data}catch(e){}e&&e.__tcfapiCall&&(e=e.__tcfapiCall,a=e.callId,s.executeTcfApi(e.command,e.parameter,function(e,t){e={__tcfapiReturn:{returnValue:e,success:t,callId:a}};i&&i.source&&i.source.postMessage&&i.source.postMessage(n?JSON.stringify(e):e,"*")},e.version))},this.executeTcfApi=function(){for(var e,t,i,a,n=[],r=0;r<arguments.length;r++)n[r]=arguments[r];if(!n.length)return s.win.__tcfapi.a||[];"setGdprApplies"===n[0]?3<n.length&&2===parseInt(n[1],10)&&"boolean"==typeof n[3]&&(s.gdprApplies=n[3],"function"==typeof n[2])&&n[2]("set",!0):(e=n[0],t=n[1],a=n[3],"function"==typeof(i=n[2])&&e&&("ping"===e?s.getPingReq(i,s.gdprApplies):s.addToQ(e,t,i,a)))},this.addToQ=function(e,t,i,a){var n=s.win.__tcfapi;n.a=n.a||[],n.a.push([e,t,i,a])},this.getPingReq=function(e,t){e({gdprApplies:t=void 0===t?void 0:t,cmpLoaded:!1,cmpStatus:"stub",apiVersion:"2.0"})},this.init()},i=new t;return e.TCF=t,e.tcfStub=i,Object.defineProperty(e,"__esModule",{value:!0}),e})({});</script>

        <!-- Teardown guard for the stub above. Outside GDPR regions the OneTrust
             SDK stub calls removeTcf(): it deletes window.__tcfapi and the
             __tcfapiLocator iframe, but never removes the message listener the
             TCF stub registered. Running the stub during parse means
             document.body is still null, so its locator iframe is created on a
             5ms retry timer that lands *after* that teardown. The page then
             advertises a locator frame with no __tcfapi behind it: ad iframes
             post __tcfapiCall, the orphaned listener runs, and addToQ throws
             "Cannot read properties of undefined (reading 'a')" once per call.
             Detach the listener and drop the orphan frame instead of throwing.
             In GDPR regions the SDK installs the real CMP and this guard hands
             the listener back to it. -->
        <script type="text/javascript">
        (function () {
            var stubHandler = window.receiveOTMessage;
            if (typeof stubHandler !== 'function' || typeof window.__tcfapi !== 'function') return;

            function detach() {
                window.removeEventListener('message', guardedHandler, false);
                var locator = document.querySelector('iframe[name="__tcfapiLocator"]');
                if (locator && locator.parentNode) locator.parentNode.removeChild(locator);
            }

            // GDPR path: intializeIabStub() overwrites window.receiveOTMessage
            // and registers its own listener without removing the stub's, so
            // both would answer every __tcfapiCall and vendor callbacks could
            // fire twice. Step aside and leave the real CMP's locator frame.
            function yieldToCmp() {
                window.removeEventListener('message', guardedHandler, false);
                clearInterval(poll);
            }

            function guardedHandler(event) {
                if (window.receiveOTMessage !== guardedHandler) {
                    yieldToCmp();
                    return;
                }
                if (typeof window.__tcfapi === 'function') {
                    stubHandler(event);
                    return;
                }
                detach();
            }

            window.removeEventListener('message', stubHandler, false);
            window.receiveOTMessage = guardedHandler;
            window.addEventListener('message', guardedHandler, false);

            // Vendors that already saw the locator frame wait for a reply that
            // can no longer come, so drop it as soon as the SDK has decided
            // rather than on the first message.
            //
            // Two consecutive misses, not one: today's SDK only assigns
            // __tcfapi when it is undefined and never deletes it on the GDPR
            // path, but it is remote code, and a single sample landing in a
            // future delete-then-reinstall gap would strip a live CMP's
            // locator frame. detach() is idempotent and the interval keeps
            // running, so a frame created by the stub's own pending
            // addFrame() retry after the teardown gets cleaned up too.
            var checks = 0;
            var misses = 0;
            var poll = setInterval(function () {
                if (window.receiveOTMessage !== guardedHandler) {
                    yieldToCmp();
                    return;
                }
                if (typeof window.__tcfapi === 'function') {
                    misses = 0;
                } else if (++misses > 1) {
                    detach();
                }
                if (++checks > 80) clearInterval(poll);
            }, 250);
        })();
        </script>

        <script type="text/javascript">
            function OptanonWrapper() {

                window.fpGetCookieGeoInfo = function() {
                    let geoInfo = {
                        isCCPA: false,
                        isGDPR: false,
                    };

                    const oneTrustGeo = window.OneTrust?.getGeolocationData();
                    const fpGdpr = FP?.readCookie('fpisgdpr');
                    const fpState = FP?.readCookie('fpdefloc');

                    // getGeolocationData() returns an ISO country code ('DE'), never 'EU',
                    // so ask OneTrust which template it applied instead: only the EU and UK
                    // rules use the IAB TCF template. That signal is ready on the first
                    // pageview, while `fpisgdpr` only exists after api/nc/defloc.php has run.
                    const oneTrustIsTcf = window.OneTrust?.GetDomainData?.()?.IabType === 'IAB2V2';

                    if (fpGdpr || oneTrustIsTcf) {
                        geoInfo.isGDPR = true;
                    }
                    if (fpState === 'CA' || (oneTrustGeo?.state === 'CA' && oneTrustGeo?.country === 'US')) {
                        geoInfo.isCCPA = true;
                    }

                    return geoInfo;
                }

                window.fpSetCookieOptout = function(groupsToCheck = []) {
                    if (!groupsToCheck || !groupsToCheck.length) return;

                    const targetedCookieGroups = ['C0002', 'C0004'];
                    let isOptedout = false;
                    targetedCookieGroups.forEach(group => {
                        if (!groupsToCheck.includes(group)) {
                            isOptedout = true;
                        }
                    });

                    window.fpCookieOptout = isOptedout;
                    if (isOptedout && !FP?.readCookie('_pbjs_id_optout') && (window?.fpCookieGeoInfo?.isGDPR || window?.fpCookieGeoInfo?.isCCPA)) {
                        // user is opted - prevents userId modules from firing
                        FP?.createCookie('_pbjs_id_optout', '1', 365);
                    }
                }

                window.fpOptoutCookieGroups = ['C0002', 'C0004'];
                window.fpCookieOptout = window.fpCookieOptout || false;
                window.fpCookieGeoInfo = window.fpCookieGeoInfo || window.fpGetCookieGeoInfo();

                if (!window.fpCookieOptout && window?.OnetrustActiveGroups) {
                    const activeGroups = window.OnetrustActiveGroups?.split(',');
                    if (activeGroups?.length) {
                        window.fpSetCookieOptout(activeGroups);
                    }
                }

                // fires when user updates their preferances
                OneTrust.OnConsentChanged(function(eventData) {
                    const activeCookieGroups = eventData?.detail ?? [];
                    if (activeCookieGroups?.length) {
                        window.fpSetCookieOptout(activeCookieGroups);
                    }
                })
            }
        </script>

                    gtag('set', 'user_id', window._fp_user.uuid);
                }
                if (window._fp_user && window._fp_user.sub_level) {

                } else {

                }

                    let ga_league_sync = 0;
                    if (window._fp_user.nfl_leagues) {
                        ga_league_sync += parseInt(window._fp_user.nfl_leagues);
                    }
                    if (window._fp_user.mlb_leagues) {

                    }
                    if (window._fp_user.nba_leagues) {
                        ga_league_sync += parseInt(window._fp_user.nba_leagues);
                    }
                    gtag('set', 'user_properties', {'league_sync': ga_league_sync});
                }
            }
        }

        function ga() {
            try {
                const args = Array.from(arguments);

                if (args[0] === 'send' && args[1] === 'event') {
                    if (!args || args.length < 4) return;

                    // Mixpanel key action click for nav links
                    if (window.mp_keyactionclick && typeof window.mp_keyactionclick === 'function') {
                        let kac_location = '';
                        if (args[2] == 'Main Nav') {
                            kac_location = 'mega_nav_legacy';
                        } else if (args[2] == 'Sub Nav') {
                            kac_location = 'sub_nav_legacy';
                        }
                        if (kac_location != '') {
                            let kac_props = {'clicked_nav_section': args[3].replace('Link Click', ''), 'clicked_nav_link_title': 'Nav Heading'};
                            if (args.length == 5) {
                                kac_props['clicked_nav_link_title'] = args[4];
                            }
                            window.mp_keyactionclick('nav_link_click', kac_location, kac_props);
                        }
                    }

                    // if (args.length === 6) {
                    //     if (typeof args[5] === 'object') {
                    //         ga_v3('send', 'event', args[2], args[3], args[4]);
                    //     } else {
                    //         ga_v3('send', 'event', args[2], args[3], args[4], args[5]);
                    //     }

                    //     ga_v3('send', 'event', args[2], args[3], args[4]);
                    // } else if (args.length === 4) {
                    //     ga_v3('send', 'event', args[2], args[3]);
                    // }

                    let gtagArgs = {};
                    args.forEach((eventValue, index) => {
                        if (index < 2) return;
                        if (!eventValue) return;

                        if (eventValue && typeof eventValue === 'object') {
                            Object.keys(eventValue).forEach(key => {
                                if (key === 'nonInteraction') {
                                    gtagArgs['non_interaction'] = eventValue[key];
                                } else {
                                    gtagArgs[key] = eventValue[key];
                                }
                            });
                        } else if (index === 2) {
                            gtagArgs['event_category'] = eventValue;
                        } else if (index === 4) {
                            gtagArgs['event_label'] = eventValue;
                        }
                    });

                    const eventAction = args[3];
                    gtag('event', eventAction, gtagArgs);
                } else if (args[0] === 'send' && args[1] === 'pageview') {

                    if (args.length === 3) {
                        // ga_v3('send', 'pageview', args[2]);
                        gtag('event', 'page_view', {
                            page_location: args[2]
                        });
                    } else {
                        // ga_v3('send', 'pageview');

                    // gtag "set" commands should be handled in gtag_user()
                    // ga_v3('set', args[1], args[2]);
                }
            } catch (e) {
                console.error(e);
            }

    // load/error is the only signal separating a live SDK from its synchronous stub.
    const tag = document.querySelector('script[src*="braze.min.js"]');
    if (tag) {
      tag.addEventListener('load', () => {
        sdkReady = true;
        if (enableIfDisabled()) {
          started = false;
          identify(pendingUuid);
        }
      });
      tag.addEventListener('error', () => { sdkReady = false; });
    }
    initializeSdk();
  }

  // "enableSDK() does not initialize the Braze SDK so you must also call initialize() afterward"
  // (https://www.braze.com/docs/developer_guide/analytics/managing_data_collection/).
  function initializeSdk() {
    // Accepted risk: campaign editors can execute same-origin JS in logged-in sessions.
    // fptoken is not HttpOnly and there is no CSP, so publish rights equal production deploy rights.
    window.braze.initialize(CFG.apiKey, {
      baseUrl: CFG.baseUrl, enableLogging: CFG.enableLogging, allowUserSuppliedJavascript: true
    });
    // Only opted-in documents may render Braze campaigns.
    if (CFG.enabled) {
      if (window.FP?.homeInterruptions) {
        // Subscribe before openSession; defer presentation, not message delivery.
        window.braze.subscribeToInAppMessage((message) => {
          // Control impressions are invisible and must not consume the interruption slot.
          if (message.isControl) return window.braze.showInAppMessage(message);
          window.FP.homeInterruptions.whenAllowed(() => {
            if (window.FP.homeInterruptions.tryClaim('braze')) window.braze.showInAppMessage(message);
          });
        });
      } else {
        window.braze.automaticallyShowInAppMessages();
      }
    }
  }

  function enableIfDisabled() {
    if (!sdkReady || optedOut() || typeof window.braze.isDisabled !== 'function') return false;
    try {
      if (window.braze.isDisabled()) {
        window.braze.enableSDK();
        initializeSdk();
        return true;
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    return false;
  }

  function identify(uuid) {
    if (!uuid || started || !CFG.enabled) return;
    ensureLoaded();
    window.braze.changeUser(uuid);
    window.braze.openSession();
    const logged = window.braze.logCustomEvent('page_view', {
      url: `${window.location.origin}${window.location.pathname}`,
      path: window.location.pathname
    });
    if (logged !== false) started = true;
  }

  function cookieNames() {
    return document.cookie.split(';').map(cookie => cookie.trim().split('=')[0]).filter(Boolean);
  }

  function expireCookie(name) {
    const deletion = `${name}=; Max-Age=0; path=/`;
    document.cookie = deletion;
    const hostParts = window.location.hostname.split('.');
    if (hostParts.length > 1) {
      document.cookie = `${deletion}; domain=.${hostParts.slice(-2).join('.')}`;
    }
  }

  const hasResidue = () => {
    try {
      for (let i = localStorage.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const key = localStorage.key(i);
        if (key && isResidueKey(key)) return true;
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    return cookieNames().some(isResidueKey);
  };

  function clearResidue() {
    try {
      for (let i = localStorage.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const key = localStorage.key(i);
        if (key && isResidueKey(key)) localStorage.removeItem(key);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    cookieNames().filter(isResidueKey).forEach(expireCookie);
  }

  function clearCampaignState() {
    try {
      for (let i = sessionStorage.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
        const key = sessionStorage.key(i);
        if (key && key.indexOf(CAMPAIGN_PREFIX) === 0) sessionStorage.removeItem(key);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    cookieNames().filter(name => name.indexOf(CAMPAIGN_PREFIX) === 0).forEach(name => {
      if (window.FP && typeof window.FP.deleteCookie === 'function') window.FP.deleteCookie(name);
      else expireCookie(name);
    });
  }

  // disableSDK() stops collection and persists that state; wipeData() clears SDK data.
  function applyConsent() {
    const out = optedOut();
    const changed = out !== wasOptedOut;
    wasOptedOut = out;
    if (out) {
      if (changed && loaded) { try { window.braze.disableSDK(); } catch (e) {} }
      if (changed && !loaded && hasResidue()) clearResidue();
      started = false;
      return;
    }
    // Re-enable before identity calls; enableSDK() alone leaves the SDK inert (docs above).
    if (changed || !started) enableIfDisabled();
    identify(pendingUuid);
  }

  function subscribeToConsentChanges() {
    if (consentSubscribed || !window.OneTrust || typeof window.OneTrust.OnConsentChanged !== 'function') return;
    consentSubscribed = true;
    window.OneTrust.OnConsentChanged(() => { window.setTimeout(applyConsent, 0); });
  }

  function awaitConsent() {
    if (consentTimer) return;
    consentStartedAt = Date.now();
    consentSlowPolling = false;
    const tick = () => {
      subscribeToConsentChanges();
      if (consentResolved()) {
        applyConsent();
        window.clearInterval(consentTimer);
        consentTimer = null;
        return;
      }
      const elapsed = Date.now() - consentStartedAt;
      if (elapsed >= CONSENT_GIVE_UP_MS) {
        window.clearInterval(consentTimer);
        consentTimer = null;
        return;
      }
      if (elapsed >= CONSENT_TIMEOUT_MS && !consentSlowPolling) {
        window.clearInterval(consentTimer);
        consentSlowPolling = true;
        consentTimer = window.setInterval(tick, CONSENT_SLOW_POLL_MS);
      }
    };
    consentTimer = window.setInterval(tick, CONSENT_POLL_MS);
    tick();
  }

  window.fpBrazeStart = function (uuid) {
    if (!uuid || !CFG.enabled) return;
    pendingUuid = uuid;
    awaitConsent();
  };

  window.fpBrazeWipe = function () {
    if (window.fpBrazeReady()) {
      try { window.braze.wipeData(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    clearResidue();
    clearCampaignState();
    started = false;
    pendingUuid = null;
  };

  document.addEventListener('click', e => {
    const anchor = e.target && e.target.closest && e.target.closest('a[href]');
    if (!anchor) return;
    try {
      if (new URL(anchor.href, window.location.href).pathname.indexOf('/accounts/signout') === 0) {
        window.fpBrazeWipe();
      }
    } catch (error) {}
  }, true);

  awaitConsent();

  // braze.php never inlines identity; fp-user.js drains this queue once _fp_user resolves.
  (window._load_user_data_callback = window._load_user_data_callback || []).push(() => {
    if (window._fp_user && window._fp_user.uuid) window.fpBrazeStart(window._fp_user.uuid);
  });
})();
</script>

            <script type="text/javascript">
            /* fp_pages counts pageviews for the fp_pageviews GPT key/value and for
               the video ad gates. It lives here, ahead of both the Freestar and
               the legacy GPT blocks, because Freestar pages skip
               templates/ads/js_googletag.php where this used to run, so the
               cookie stopped being written once Freestar went site-wide.

               The window is 3 days, up from the 1 day js_googletag.php used. */
            (function () {
                var views = Math.min(20, (parseInt(FP.readCookie('fp_pages'), 10) || 0) + 1);

                FP.createCookie('fp_pages', views, 3);
                window.FP_PAGEVIEWS = views;
            })();

            /* CLS: Freestar's sticky footer (adhesion) slides up into view, and each
               frame of that movement counts as a layout shift of the whole unit.
               Pin it to the bottom and kill the animation so it renders in place.
               Freestar hides it on close via display, so the pin is safe. */
            #fs-sticky-footer,
            #fs-sticky-footer .fs-sticky-wrapper {
                animation: none !important;
                transition: none !important;
            }
            #fs-sticky-footer { bottom: 0 !important; }

            /* Crop sticky footer creatives to the placement height; the slot, not the fixed wrapper, so the close button stays visible. */
            #fs-sticky-footer .fs-sticky-slot-element {
                max-height: 50px;
                overflow: hidden;
            }
            @media (min-width: 731px) {
                #fs-sticky-footer .fs-sticky-slot-element { max-height: 90px; }
            }

            /* Third-party ad partners (e.g. Big Happy's #bh-wrapper-iframe) inject a wrapper div holding an
               iframe directly under <body>, outside any Freestar slot, so the data-freestar-ad size caps can't
               reach them. Crop any such wrapper on mobile. The cap goes on the wrapper, since overflow has no
               effect on an iframe itself. Our own interstitial and any dialog/modal are exempt. */
            @media (max-width: 730px) {
                #bh-wrapper-iframe,
                body > div:has(> iframe):not(.fp-interstitial):not([role="dialog"]):not([aria-modal]) {
                    max-height: 50px;
                    overflow: hidden;
                }
            }

            /* Big Happy's escaped creative (#bh-wrapper-iframe and its .bh-div-wrapper click overlay) stacks over our
               modals. Keep it visible but pin it to z-index 2: above the mobile rankings sticky player columns (1), below
               every modal (the lowest, FpModal's backdrop, is 10). The iframe is included in case the wrapper itself isn't
               positioned. In-slot creatives render inside the ad iframe's own document, so these only match the escaped
               copy. !important beats the ad script's inline styles. */
            #bh-wrapper-iframe,
            .bh-div-wrapper,
            iframe[src*="bighappy.co"] {
                z-index: 2 !important;
            }

            /* CLS: when a creative renders, Freestar bumps the slot min-height past
               the value its cls.css reserved (320x50 -> 62px, 728x90 -> 104px for
               its branding bar). Pre-reserve the final size so the slot doesn't
               grow after first paint. 300x250 slots don't get the bump; other
               sizes (320x100, 160x600) are unmeasured, so they're deliberately
               not reserved here — measure before adding, since over-reserving
               shows permanent whitespace on units without the bump. */
            [data-freestar-ad="__320x50"] { min-height: 62px; }
            [data-freestar-ad="__728x90"],
            [data-freestar-ad="__970x90"] { min-height: 104px; }

                <script data-cfasync="false" type="text/javascript">
            // Freestar configures the STN slider video with parentElement "body". Their video
            // teardown (TTD protection -> _suppressVideo -> RT) does parent.innerHTML = "",
            // which deletes the whole page. Block that one write until they repoint the unit.
            // Remove this once fantasypros_stn_slider has a real container element.
            (function () {
                try {
                    var desc = Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(Element.prototype, 'innerHTML');
                    if (!desc || !desc.set) return;
                    Object.defineProperty(HTMLBodyElement.prototype, 'innerHTML', {
                        configurable: true,
                        get: desc.get,
                        set: function (value) {
                            if (value === '' && this === document.body) {
                                window.fpBlockedBodyWipe = (window.fpBlockedBodyWipe || 0) + 1;
                                if (window.console && console.warn) {
                                    console.warn('[FP] blocked document.body.innerHTML = "" (Freestar STN slider teardown)');
                                }
                                return;
                            }
                            desc.set.call(this, value);
                        }
                    });
                } catch (e) {}
            })();
        </script>
        
                <script src="https://a.pub.network/fantasypros-com/pubfig.min.js" data-cfasync="false" async></script>
                            <script>
                // STN slider video, gated five ways:
                //   - tablet & desktop only (>=768), not mobile
                //   - logged out only. fp_level holds base64 of one of the
                //     valid tiers, decoded and checked the same way
                //     includes/initialize.php and templates/article_single.php
                //     do it, so the two video gates agree on who is logged in.
                //   - from the 3rd pageview on. FP_PAGEVIEWS is the fp_pages
                //     cookie after this pageview is counted, so 3 here is a
                //     stored value of 2 before the increment.
                //   - current browsers and operating systems only. The block
                //     below sets oldClient and explains each version it checks.
                //   - not iOS/iPadOS 16-19, per Freestar: the STN slider
                //     misbehaves on those releases.
                (function () {
                    var fpLoggedIn = false;
                    try {
                        fpLoggedIn = ['basic', 'pro', 'mvp', 'hof', 'goat'].indexOf(atob(FP.readCookie('fp_level') || '')) !== -1;
                    } catch (e) {}

                    var ua = navigator.userAgent;

                    /* Each number is the last version of that browser that runs
                       on Windows 7, 8 and 8.1. Edge and Opera need no rows of
                       their own: their user agents carry a Chrome token holding
                       the Chromium version they are built on, so the Chrome row
                       catches them. */
                    var browserFloors = [
                        [/Firefox\/(\d+)/, 115],
                        [/Chrome\/(\d+)/, 109]
                    ];
                    var oldBrowser = false;

                    for (var i = 0; i < browserFloors.length; i++) {
                        var hit = browserFloors[i][0].exec(ua);
                        if (hit) {
                            oldBrowser = parseInt(hit[1], 10) <= browserFloors[i][1];
                            break;
                        }
                    }

                    /* Windows NT 6.3, 6.2 and 6.1 are Windows 8.1, 8 and 7.
                       Mac OS X 10_14 and below is macOS Mojave and earlier.

                       Both fields are frozen on newer releases - Chrome reports
                       Windows NT 10.0 from Chrome 110 on, Apple reports Mac OS X
                       10_15_7 from macOS 11 on - which costs nothing, because
                       nothing that new runs on the releases being excluded. It
                       does put Catalina on the far side of Apple's freeze, so
                       10.15 passes.

                       Safari and Chrome write 10_14_6, Firefox writes 10.14. The
                       iOS "like Mac OS X" string carries no version after it and
                       never matches. */
                    var winNt = /Windows NT (\d+)/.exec(ua);
                    var macOs = /Mac OS X 10[._](\d+)/.exec(ua);
                    var oldClient = oldBrowser
                        || (!!winNt && parseInt(winNt[1], 10) < 10)
                        || (!!macOs && parseInt(macOs[1], 10) < 15);

                    // iPhone writes "iPhone OS 17_0", iPad writes "CPU OS 17_0"; neither reaches macOS.
                    var blockedIos = /OS 1[6-9]_/i.test(ua);

                    if (window.innerWidth >= 768 && !fpLoggedIn && (window.FP_PAGEVIEWS || 1) >= 3 && !oldClient && !blockedIos) {
                        window.freestar.queue.push(function(){
                            // One slider per page: a second newVideo() stacks another player on body.
                            if (window.fpStnSliderLaunched || typeof window.freestar.newVideo !== 'function') return;
                            window.fpStnSliderLaunched = true;
                            window.freestar.newVideo("fantasypros_stn_slider");
                        });
                    }
                })();
            </script>

    function loadTableAds() {
        if (typeof window._fp_level !== 'undefined' && (window._fp_level === '' || window._fp_level === 'basic')) {
            googletag.cmd.push(function() {
            $width = window.innerWidth;
        if (($width >= 1200 && $width < 1400) || ($width >= 900 && $width < 1080) || $width < 825) {
                    var ad_codes = JSON.parse('[]');
                    for (var i in ad_codes) {
                        var slot = googletag.defineSlot(ad_codes[i]['url'], [ad_codes[i]['size'].split(',').map(Number)], 'div-gpt-ad-'+ad_codes[i]['id'])
                                            .addService(googletag.pubads());
                        googletag.display('div-gpt-ad-'+ad_codes[i]['id']);
                        if (typeof ad_codes[i]['omitRefresh'] === 'undefined') {
                            googletag.pubads().refresh([slot]);
                        }
                        tableAds++;
                    }
                }
                    });
        }
    }

    function tableAdSortRow(sortList, index, order) {
        if (!sortList || sortList[0][0] !== index || sortList[0][1] !== order) {
            document.querySelectorAll(".ad-row").forEach(function(ad) { ad.style.display = "none" });
        }
    }

<script type="text/javascript">
    // Client-side so cached HTML can't pin one variant. Must run before the deferred
    // fp-events.js (loaded in h_open.php) fires Pageview. The banner reads the same global.
    window._fp_polymarket_variant = Math.random() < 0.5 ? 'v1' : 'v2';
    if (window._mp_pageview_options) {
        window._mp_pageview_options['creative_variant'] = window._fp_polymarket_variant;
    }
</script>

<script type="text/javascript">
    // tickers.js owns the creative but has no access to partner_windows(); this keeps
    // the flight window in PHP so the ticker and the Vue slot gate cannot disagree.
    window.ticker_toyota_ecr = true;
</script>

<script>
(function () {
    try {
        const get = function (n) {
            const m = document.cookie.match(new RegExp('(?:^|; )' + n + '=([^;]*)'));
            return m ? decodeURIComponent(m[1]) : null;
        };
        const set = function (n, v, maxAge) { 
            let domain = '.fantasypros.com';
            const hostSplit = location.host.split('.');
            const tld = hostSplit[hostSplit.length - 1];
            if (tld === 'dev') domain = '.fp.dev';
            if (tld === 'test') domain = '.fp.test';
            document.cookie = n + '=' + v + '; path=/; domain=' + domain + '; max-age=' + maxAge + '; Secure; SameSite=None';
        };
        const THIRTY_DAYS = 60 * 60 * 24 * 30;

        // Registered users don't participate — reset the counter so it can't leak into a session.
        if (get('fptoken')) {
            if (get('fp_rankings_visits') !== null) set('fp_rankings_visits', '', 0);
            if (get('fp_ecr_pos_fence_26') !== null) set('fp_ecr_pos_fence_26', '', 0);
            if (get('fp_weekly_ecr_wr_26') !== null) set('fp_weekly_ecr_wr_26', '', 0);
            return;
        }

        // 1-based inclusive visit count (all sports). ?fp_rankings_visits=N overrides for QA.
        const forcedVisits = new URLSearchParams(location.search).get('fp_rankings_visits');
        const visits = forcedVisits !== null
            ? parseInt(forcedVisits, 10) || 0
            : (parseInt(get('fp_rankings_visits'), 10) || 0) + 1;
        set('fp_rankings_visits', visits, THIRTY_DAYS);

        // CFP-1181: the retired bucket cookie is nobody's input any more — expire it so it
        // stops riding along for the 30 days its last write bought.
        if (get('fp_ecr_pos_fence_26') !== null) set('fp_ecr_pos_fence_26', '', 0);
        // CFP-1365: same for the concluded weekly WR 50/50 -- prior no-fence users now fence.
        if (get('fp_weekly_ecr_wr_26') !== null) set('fp_weekly_ecr_wr_26', '', 0);


        // Stamp the landing Pageview (fires later, from deferred fp-events.js). The visit count
        // is sport-agnostic like the counter above, but the fence property is NFL-only: it must
        // describe the population the fence can actually reach ($app_settings['pos_fence_modal']
        // below is set for NFL alone). While it was a 50/50 the value at least identified an arm;
        // as a constant on every sport it would only inflate the denominator and break the pre/post
        // comparison it is kept around for. Mirrors how App.vue stamps fp_ecr_rb_wr_26 only when
        // the combo is fence-eligible.
        if (window._mp_pageview_options) {
            window._mp_pageview_options.rankings_visit_count = visits;
            window._mp_pageview_options.fp_ecr_pos_fence_26 = 'show-fence';
        }
    } catch (e) { /* never block the page on the counter */ }
})();
</script>

    let registrationData = {"id":"registration-fence","header":"Create a free account to unlock","subtitle":"Sign up today to access these rankings and unlock a more powerful experience.","is_visible":true,"is_registration":true,"title":"Create A Free Account","description":"<strong>Sign Up for Free and Unlock Pro Features!<\/strong>","list_items":[[],[],[]],"cta_link":"\/premium\/plans\/","cta_button_text":"Create An Account","learn_more_link":null,"screenshot_url":"","is_premium_feature":true,"test_name":null,"ga_event":{"category":"","action":"","label":""},"registration_module":"\n            <div class=\"report-page-fence\">\n                <div class=\"report-fence-container\">\n                        <div id=\"registration-module\"><\/div>\n                <\/div>\n            <\/div>"};
    window.registrationFence = window.registrationFence || {};
    window.registrationFence.data = registrationData;
    // The bundle below is async, so the real showRegistrationModal() -- installed by
    // RegistrationModal.vue on mount -- may not exist for a while yet. Several
    // templates wire the bare global straight onto a fenced control (nfl_site_rankings,
    // nfl_sleepers_busts, mlb_sleepers_busts, article_category), and those controls
    // render only when the fence does, so the click target is live from first paint.
    // Record the request instead of throwing ReferenceError; index.js replays it on
    // mount. Guarded so a second fence include cannot clobber the real implementation.
    window.showRegistrationModal = window.showRegistrationModal || function () {
        window.registrationFence.pendingOpen = true;
    };
    </script>

        function handleSkipNavClick() {
            const customSkipContainerId = 'skip-nav-to-this-content';
            const customSkipContainerEl = document.getElementById(customSkipContainerId);

            if(customSkipContainerEl) {
                window.location.hash = `#${customSkipContainerId}`;
                customSkipContainerEl.focus()
            } 
        }

        function removeTopBannerElements() {
            var bannerElementsToRemove = document.querySelectorAll('#ticker-bar, #banner-styles, #banner-home-styles');
            if (bannerElementsToRemove && bannerElementsToRemove.length) {
                bannerElementsToRemove.forEach(function(elem) {
                    elem.parentNode.removeChild(elem);
                });
            }
            document.body.classList.remove('fp-has-top-ticker');
        }

        let showTopTicker = FP.readCookie('fp-ticker-dismissed') !== 'true';
        let showPromoBar = false;
        let showAlertBanner = false;

        let alertBannerIds = [];
                let allAlertBannerIds = [];
        if (Array.isArray(allAlertBannerIds)) {
            allAlertBannerIds.forEach(tickerId => {
                if (!FP.readCookie(`fp-alert-ticker-dismissed-${tickerId}`)) {
                    alertBannerIds.push(tickerId);
                }
            });

            // Show banner if any alert banners exist, even if promo banner has been dismissed
            if (alertBannerIds.length) {
                showAlertBanner = true;
            }
        }

                }

                showPromoBar = true;
            }
